- `readStripeKeysFromInstall(config: Record<string, unknown>): StripeKeys` — Read Ed's keys off the plugin install config. Never logs them.
- `stripeConfigured(config: Record<string, unknown> | undefined | null): boolean` — Cheap "is Stripe set up?" check for the UI — true when a secret key is present.

- `normaliseLocale(input: string | null | undefined): string | null` — Lowercase the language subtag, uppercase the region, drop all but the first two subtags. `EN-us` → `en-US`, `FR` → `fr`, garbage → null.

If no prefix matches, returns null. Compares locales…
- `parseAcceptLanguage(header: string | null | undefined): string[]` — Parses an HTTP `Accept-Language` header into a quality-sorted list of locale tags. Malformed entries are skipped silently.
- `resolveLocale(input: ResolveLocaleInput, page: LocalePageMap): ResolveLocaleResult` — Pick the locale to serve. Priority: 1. explicit override (cookie / query) 2. URL prefix 3. Accept-Language header (first match — exact, then language-only) 4. defaultLocale If the…
- `localizedTree(page: LocalePageMap, locale: string): LocalizedTreeResult`
- `localizedUrl(pathname: string, locale: string, defaultLocale: string): string` — Produces a `/<locale>/<path>` URL — used by the editor language picker and storefront `<link rel="alternate" hreflang>` emission. The default locale renders WITHOUT a prefix (`/ab…
- `buildHreflangLinks(pathname: string, page: LocalePageMap, origin: string): string` — Build `<link rel="alternate" hreflang="…">` tags for the page across every available locale + the `x-default` reference. Storefront stamps these into <head>.

- `pageAllowsRoleAt(plugin: AquaPlugin, page: PluginPage, host: HostSurface, role: Role): boolean` — The single call a host route makes after resolving a URL to a page.

- `effectiveApiRoles(plugin: AquaPlugin, route: PluginApiRoute): Role[]` — Exactly who this route may answer. `[]` means nobody with a session. `public: true` routes are outside the role system entirely — they are the ones that land as an anonymous visit…
